RPHELPER FOREVER LEGACY AND POTENTIALLY INHERITED CONTENT INVENTORY
Generated: 2026-10-23

PURPOSE AND SCOPE

This is a reference inventory, not a runtime data file or a license grant.
It records every active built-in reaction, every non-empty archived/deferred
reaction, and every random-insult fragment currently shipped by RPHelper
Forever. Because current files mix migrated, adapted, and newly authored
material, entries without an exact historical match are conservatively marked
uncertain and included as potentially inherited. Empty ability definitions and
empty archive tables contain no creative reaction wording and are not entries.

Each text value is represented as a Lua-style quoted string so quotation marks,
backslashes, tabs, and line breaks remain unambiguous. historical-exact means
the complete current text occurs in the named historical source.
historical-placeholder-adapted means it matches after removing Forever's braces
from uppercase template keywords. uncertain means no such match was found; it
does not assert that the entry is new or identify an author.

PRIMARY HISTORICAL SOURCES

- RPHelper 2 extracted distribution: /home/aristeidis/Downloads/RPHelper2/
- RPHelper 2 original archive: /home/aristeidis/Downloads/RPHelper2.zip
- Turtle WoW adaptation: ../RPhelper_twow/English/
